This versioned contract qualifies exact reviewed repository source. It grants no runtime authority, account activation, credential access, deployment permission, or finding allowance beyond the eighteen enumerated structural observations.

Serialization is ast.parse(type_comments=True), ast.dump(annotate_fields=True, include_attributes=False, indent=2), UTF-8 without trailing newline, uppercase SHA256. Existing fingerprint algorithms are unchanged.

Eleven full-module dependency guards are prerequisites, never exemptions. VR1 permits new UUID4 disabled organization INSERT, not takeover/update. VR2 changes supplemental scope versions; it is not a blanket pending-only contract. VR3 permits member/pending membership and initial assignment/binding entries with inactive account, disabled organization and final effective scope zero, preserving existing membership roles. Current DB-admin, CSRF, confirmation, signed actor/target/TTL, CAS, same-vendor/site and transaction guards remain required. Preview projection is readonly; fixture bootstrap separately writes synthetic data.

The original observation remains bound to 11a43364: 281 add attempts, 210 legacy consumptions, 71 appended occurrences and 18 logical findings. A fresh candidate qualification compares the complete independent VR multiset to that accepted ledger; it never refreshes the baseline. VR events never enter legacy StructuralAllowance sets, never change created_tables/residual scans, and never alter traversal or dedup. Only exact full equality permits the separate final filter; direct append and all remaining findings retain failure semantics.

Three reference streams are independent: 903 original candidates, 151 adapter nodes and 50 tool nodes. They overlap and are not 1104 unique references. Exact owner/node fingerprints and canonical targets are mandatory. The twenty explicitly recorded unsupported legacy nodes have thirteen absent app bindings and remain target_source_resolved=false, outside VR authority qualification. They are accepted source observations only, not VR allowances; no re-export or runtime success/failure is inferred. Their absence in the pinned app and exact recorded shapes must remain unchanged. New/changed targets, bindings, aliases, stores, deletes, escapes or unsupported shapes reject; no scanner finding is suppressed by a reference disposition.

Only the four recorded app.app.app_context terminals are accepted external source assumptions (progress main52, settings main32, sheets main40, users main43). CLI legal modes are no arguments, --self-test, --vr-compat-r01, --vr-compat-r01-self-test, standalone -h or --help. All other arrays reject before analysis with fixed stderr, exit2 and no PASS. Counts use canonical ASCII decimal; 00 is invalid. Compatibility PASS is source qualification only, never live activation or release readiness.
